# Comment and documentation truth audit, wave 2026-11-16

A read-only audit of the whole repo at `af761b32` (= origin/main), run after the slices A47–A53 of
docs/audits/2026-11-12-comment-and-doc-truth-audit.md were merged. Its findings are the input of the slices A60–A64
in docs/plans/2026-10-30-performance-backlog.md. Nothing was fixed by the audit.

## Method

1. **Read (wave 1).** 60 Haiku agents, read-only: 49 over the source files with comments (src/, scripts/, tests/,
   fixtures/apps, docs/truth/*.mts), split by comment lines plus a share of code lines; 4 over the present-tense docs
   (README, SETUP, ARCHITECTURE, AGENTS, CLAUDE, docs/release.md, docs/verification.md, scripts/differential/README.md,
   skill/, docs/truth/calibration.md, docs/mutation-ledger.md); 6 over the ADRs (Status lines, passages contradicting
   their own amendments, present-tense claims, dead pointers only); 1 mechanical sweep for backtick identifiers, file
   paths and caller phrases in comments. 661 findings, 651 after merging duplicates. The open findings of the
   2026-11-12 audit and the open rows A54, A55, A57, A58, A59 were excluded.
2. **Gap read.** The wave-1 agents named 94 files they had not read whole; 21 Haiku agents read them, 433 findings.
3. **Verify (wave 2).** Sonnet agents re-read every finding against the code: 1084 verdicts, every finding has one.
   Wave 1: 497 confirmed, 98 partly, 56 refuted. Gap read: 378 confirmed, 33 partly, 22 refuted.

## What is not established

- Verification judged only what the readers reported; a false comment no reader reported is not here. The first audit's
  slices each found statements their audit had missed, so expect more.
- The verifiers did not know the decisions the A53 review took for tests/ (labels kept where the audit fix text keeps
  them, sentences whose subject is the review, live-records provenance, F263). A slice re-reads each finding against
  those decisions before acting.
- Line numbers are at `af761b32`; they move.

## Counts (confirmed + partly)

| Slice | Scope | Findings | high | medium |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| A60 | comments of src/ | 181 | 2 | 30 |
| A61 | comments of scripts/ and fixtures/apps | 160 | 0 | 20 |
| A62 | comments of tests/ | 337 | 1 | 33 |
| A63 | docs and ADRs | 51 | 0 | 11 |
| A64 | strings, error texts and test titles | 277 | 0 | 10 |
| | total | 1006 | 3 | 104 |

Kinds: string-or-title 277, review-label 252, history-as-today 122, caller-list 98, stale-reference 70, false-claim 69, contradiction 42, unsure-claim 39, restates-code 34, line-pointer 2, unclear-wording 1.
